- **code-review**: Single-pass code review of the current branch's diff. The reviewer comes from the `reviewer` role in ACS (Models → Roles), each family at its own flavor effort; name any family, "premium", "both" or "claude" (the multi-agent team) to choose for one run. Every finding must quote the code it cites, checked by script. Every HIGH and MEDIUM goes to one fix round without asking, applied by the builder that wrote the branch (or the agent, when no builder did); a fix check follows once, what it leaves is deferred, and the outcome is logged and reported once. The script never fixes; no loop. ([crisnc100/agent-config-studio](https://github.com/crisnc100/agent-config-studio/tree/HEAD/packs/review-gate/skills/claude/code-review) · ★2)
